- [x] AG.4 The physics seam (design decision 3), on nothing in flight: avian3d 0.8 from crates.io in `guacamole`, or Avian's `main` at `1070759b2145` pinned by rev if 0.8 hasn't published when this box starts (the reason written here), never `34c9b008d2f4`; the new pure crate `crates/guac-motion` (no Bevy) holding the seam (a shape cast returning the time of impact and the contact planes there, contact manifolds within a distance at a pose, a ray cast returning the triangle it hit, overlaps) and its parry3d implementation over static trimeshes with internal-edge fixing and the four convex shapes; `guacamole`'s Avian implementation over `SpatialQuery`, with static trimesh colliders built with `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES`, kinematic and sensor colliders and no dynamic body. Unit tests in this box, every case through BOTH implementations, Avian's in a headless app (`MinimalPlugins` and Avian's plugins, no renderer): a sphere cast onto a plane stops at the expected fraction; a corner yields two planes; a ray reports its triangle's index; a sphere sliding across the seam between two coplanar triangles never snags. Verify: record `.githooks/pre-push` cold and warm on the Mac beside AA.10's numbers, the load average beside them (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: the seam landed, both sides answer the table alike to the bit. Pin: `cargo search avian3d` on 2026-10-19 still tops out at 0.7.0 (Bevy 0.19), so `guacamole` takes Avian's `main` at `1070759b2145dc078abedace91dc7fe78013a45c` ("Update to Bevy 0.20 (#1084)", avian3d 0.8.0-dev on parry3d 0.31.1 and `bevy_transform_interpolation` 0.6) by rev, features `3d` and `parry-f32` only, until 0.8 publishes. `crates/guac-motion` (pure, `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]`, parry3d 0.31 and glam 0.33, no Bevy): `seam` holds `PhysicsSeam`: `cast` (a convex shape swept along a motion, unturned: the fraction and meters to the first solid it moves into, and the contact planes within the distance asked at that pose, never less than `CAST_SLOP` 1e-3 m so the hit is among them), `contacts` (one plane per body and triangle within a distance, its deepest point: body, triangle, material, normal out of the body, point, signed gap), `ray` (body, triangle, material, distance, point, normal; both sides of a triangle; out of a convex it starts inside) and `overlaps` (sensors included), each with an exclude list; bodies static, kinematic or sensor (overlaps only); the four `CollisionInfo` shapes, box and cylinder rounded by a margin (Havok's convex radius); a `Mesh` with a material per triangle, refused by name when empty, its materials miscounted, an index out of range or a vertex not finite. `parry` holds `ParryWorld`, shapes built as Avian builds them (its `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES` is parry's plus `ORIENTED`, its capsule +Y to −Y) and every query the parry call `SpatialQuery` makes with Avian's cast options (unit direction, origin penetration ignored), a per-body AABB cull; `set_pose` moves a kinematic body or sensor and refuses a static one. `guacamole`'s [`physics.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/physics.rs): `SeamPlugin` adds `PhysicsSchedulePlugin`, `ColliderHierarchyPlugin`, `ColliderBackendPlugin`, `ColliderTreePlugin` and `BroadPhaseCorePlugin` (its system sets only) and puts `SolverSystems::Finalize` in `PhysicsStepSystems::Solver`, no solver, narrow phase, pairs, joints, mass, interpolation or transform sync; the first try without the last two didn't build (Avian builds `PhysicsSchedule` with ambiguity detection as an error: 5 ambiguous pairs), and without the hierarchy plugin a body's collider never joined a tree (`update_moved_collider_aabbs` panicked on its placeholder index). `spawn_body`: the mesh `RigidBody::Static` on `Collider::trimesh_with_config(.., FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES)`, a placement `Kinematic`, a sensor `Kinematic` with `Sensor`, none dynamic, each posed by `Position` and `Rotation`; `AvianSeam`, a `SystemParam`: casts through `cast_shape_predicate`, rays through `cast_ray_predicate` with the triangle from the same ray on the hit collider's parry shape (`RayHitData` has no feature), contacts from `aabb_intersections_with_aabb` and parry's manifolds on `Collider::shape_scaled` (Avian's `contact_manifolds` drops the sub-shape, the triangle), overlaps through `shape_intersections`; a moved body shows once the physics schedule has run. Registered in the TV app (`mod physics`, `physics::SeamPlugin`; `dead_code` expected outside tests until AG.16 and AG.21 call it). What it adds to startup, a headless `MinimalPlugins` app with and without it: systems 11 → 25, observers 0 → 20, resources 13 → 30, schedules 8 → 10, the physics schedule run once a fixed step; building the app 4-21 ms and an empty update 0.34-0.83 ms either way in a debug build at a 1-minute load of ~40, noise over any difference; no other entity gets a physics component. Tolerances declared in `conformance.rs` before the first run: 1e-4 against a closed form (m, and per normal component), 1e-5 between the two sides, exact on bodies, triangles, materials, plane counts and overlap sets. The first run held 1e-4 on contacts and rays but not on casts: a sphere onto the rounded door stopped 1.57e-4 m past it (1.700157 for 1.7), so cast distances check to 1e-3 (`TOL_CAST`); a grazing cast across the seam from 5 mm inside stopped at 0.8890 m against the closed form's 0.9003 (1.2 mm of gap at an 8° graze), checked as a bound; and exactly along a face it started 0.1 m inside, a cast stopped at 0, float noise in the contact normal deciding, so that query became slightly-away and `cast`'s docs say it. Otherwise the casts sat within 2.0e-5 (sphere onto plane) and 1.3e-5 (the shapes) of closed form. Avian against parry: all 107 answers bit-identical, 87 planes among them, so none of the declared 1e-5 is used. The table, 16 cases, 107 queries, each case through both (`guac-motion`'s `the_table`, `guacamole`'s `the_table_through_avian_agrees_with_parry` in a headless `MinimalPlugins` app, no renderer, one 1/64 s fixed step an update): `sphere_cast_onto_plane` (fraction 0.5, the floor's triangle 1 and material 7 at the stop with no contact distance asked); `convex_shapes_onto_plane` (box with and without margin, turned 0.7 rad, capsule upright and on its side, cylinder with and without margin, each stopping on its lowest point); `corner_gives_two_planes` (a floor and wall in one mesh: a sphere in the corner gets two planes, +Y and −X, and a diagonal cast stops at 0.5 touching both); `ray_reports_its_triangle` (a 32-triangle soup at level 213's body translation, each triangle its own vertices so merging rewrites the index buffer: triangles 0, 5, 17 and 31 with their materials); `seam_never_snags` (63 poses across the seam of two coplanar triangles at the original's 0.01 m snap height, touching and 5 mm inside: every plane within 0.05 m is the faces' +Y; casts across the seam and along it at the snap height go all the way); `cast_inside_doesnt_weld` (the limit above, pinned: from inside, the cast stops on the far triangle's edge while the planes there are faces'); `kinematic_blocks` and `kinematic_moved` (a door turned 90° and moved 2 m); `sensors_only_overlap` (overlaps see a sensor, casts, rays and contacts pass it); `exclude_skips_bodies`; `start_inside` (into a wall: fraction 0 at −0.1; out, or along and slightly out: no stop); `contact_reach` (a 0.03 m gap in 0.05, not in 0.02); `deepest_point` (a tipped box: the plane at −0.01, not the high edge's +0.04); `mesh_back` (contacts one-sided: a sphere under the floor gets no plane; a cast up into it stops at 1.5 m with no plane at all, written into `Cast`'s and `Mesh`'s docs for AG.15's winding and AG.16); `ray_sides`; `zero_motion`. Beside it: `guac-motion` 5 tests (the table; a world built twice answers bit for bit; the floor without internal-edge fixing gives a sphere 5 mm inside it, just short of the seam, a plane tilted against its motion (normal x under −0.05; the seam case's pose 5 read −0.23 that way), fixed under 1e-6; `ParryWorld`'s and `Mesh`'s refusals); `guacamole` 6 (the table and agreement; a move shows after the physics step and not before; Avian's markers (static mesh with `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES` and `ORIENTED`, kinematic, kinematic `Sensor`); `set_pose` refusing a static body; another entity's `Transform` untouched with no `Position`, `Rotation` or `ColliderAabb`; Avian's constructors and the parry shapes giving the same AABB for all six convex shapes). Each check deleted or bent in turn: on the Avian side 10, 9 failing the table and `Sensor` left off surviving it (the seam filters sensors by its own kind), so the markers test pins it; on the parry side 10, all failing once `deepest_point` caught the one that kept the shallowest point. Verify: `.githooks/pre-push`, cold (a fresh scratch `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`, deleted after): 218 s wall, fmt 1 s, check 51 s, clippy 32 s, test 134 s (1,167 passed, 105 ignored), at a 1-minute load of 10.7-18.2 (mean 13.0), the target 12 GB; warm in the worktree's own target: no-op 36 s (check 1, clippy 0, test 34) at 8.1-9.1, after touching `guac-motion/src/seam.rs` 42 s (test 39) at 9.1-15.4. AA.10's (2026-10-18): cold 106 and 101 s with guacamole (check 27, clippy 18, test 61 and 55), +35 to +42 s over `--exclude guacamole`, no-op 18 s, a touched `guac-server` 27 s, the target 11 GB. Each side broken in turn: Avian's cast distance ×1.01 failed `guacamole`'s table alone (8 cases off closed form), +5e-5 m (inside `TOL_CAST`, past `AGREE`) failed it on agreement alone (all 10 cases whose cast stops); parry's ×1.01 failed `guac-motion`'s table and, through the reference answers, `guacamole`'s, as it has to; the shared expectation bent (fraction 0.6) failed both; internal-edge fixing off failed `seam_never_snags` at pose 5 (normal (−0.232, 0.945, 0.232)) on each side alone, plus the markers test on Avian's. Spec and design corrections below. **The rule** ([`guac_formats::level::at_start`](../../../crates/guac-formats/src/level.rs), `ActivityState`, `AtStart`): `_activityState` takes the EBOOT's `PLACEMENT_*` order, each value seen where its name says: the hero's own placement reads 0 (PLAYER); a level file holds only 4 (UNMANAGED) and 5 (TEMPLATE); at run time a 4 becomes 1 (ACTIVE: it has a corpus, the scene-graph node its model hangs under and the holder of its phantom), 2 (INACTIVE: no corpus, so no solid, and by AF.9's layout nothing drawn) or 3 (REMOVED). A template never runs itself; an UNMANAGED placement with an activation range is active exactly while the hero stands within that range of its `_initMatrix` position, measured in 3D, the range in feet; one without a range is its scripts' to wake, take off or leave. Live, F0 of AF's fresh state, all 1,186 placements of section 0 (997 actors, 188 emitters, the light): templates 571 (413 actors, 157 emitters, the light) all live 5; ranged, in range 55 actors and 1 emitter live 1, out of range 381 actors and 30 emitters live 2, the closest calls 0.154 m inside (`Moths Cirdling lamp lrg(3)`, 24.23 m of its 24.38) and 0.479 m outside (`Coin_spinning`; measured from the camera instead, 15 miss, and across the ground only, 6); without a range, 31 live 1, 87 live 2 and 28 live 3 (`_Start_level`, `_Level_Definition`, the init and controller scripts, ten flockers); exceptions, 2 in range their own scripts took off (`Eon_1_Move_along_path`, 5.57 m from the hero, and `213_UI_Controller`, 0.00 m). Every active actor's corpus is on the game's actor list and no other placement has one. First the metric extractors in `guac-oracle`, each over a trace keyed on game time: top speed per game second, time to 90 % of it, stop distance, a reversal's stop, turn and go phases, turn rate, wall-slide speed, the camera's trail angle, eye distance and settle, and a view switch's start and length; unit tests in this box on synthetic traces with known answers, a hitch included. Then O5 recordings from AF's fresh state, every AF.7 script mapped to game time from its recorded steps, the clip and its time recorded throughout: walks from rest at full and half stick with the stick 0°, 14° and 16° from his facing (frames 1-3's heading and speed settle the 15° gate's boundary, and the acceleration law apart from it); stops; 180° reversals; turns at full and part stick and a stick let go mid-turn (the 45° gate); the stick sweep: per axis the bytes 128 ± 31, 32 and 33 and the ends 0 and 255, eight directions at strengths 0.2-1.0, and off-axis angles 10°, 22.5° and 30°, recording walk direction against stick angle and top speed per direction from the guest's readback and the controller's stick floats (which floats the stick feeds and their signs); Eruptor's live `ActorParameters`, `CollisionInfo` sphere and scale, and the oracle figure's (`512d6e36…`) hat and speed heroics. Then hero-movement's The stick walks the hero, Top speed, Speeding up and slowing down, and Turning and hard reversals, and pad-input's dead zone, written with their numbers in our own words from the traces, the decompile only explaining them. Verify: each of those requirements names its recording here; an O5 from-rest recording's distance reproduced from its own step sequence under AG.1's integration order; every place the read model misses recorded (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: the read model isn't what walks Eruptor on open ground, and the four requirements and the dead zone are rewritten from what does, every number off O5b recordings through the repo's own extractors. **The extractors** ([`guac_oracle::motion`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/motion.rs)): a `Motion` is samples keyed on game time (pivot, facing, the stick driving the next step, the camera), from an O5 `Recording` under AG.1's alignment or written by a fixed-step runner, a position between samples linear in time and a speed a step's distance over its game time; over it `top_speed` (ground and 3D path per game second), `time_to_speed`, `distance`, `stop` (distance, time, drift), `reversal` (the least speed, the facing's time round, and the stop, the turn where he stands and the go when he has them), `turn_rate` (above a wobble threshold), `wall_slide` (speed along, gap, rise), `trail` (the hero's bearing off the camera's heading, eye distance), `settle` and `view_switch`; `script_times` places an AF.7 script's port-0 steps on a recording by flip and checks each against the floats. A record holds pad 0's floats one game frame late (taken as the pad read starts, before the game reads that frame's stick), so a sample's stick is the next record's: at every one of 795 script steps over the 79 recordings below the floats change exactly one record after the step's flip. 11 tests on synthetic traces with known answers, 8 with a 0.05 s hitch (guac-oracle 49 -> 60); 16 mutations each fail one, two only after a case was added. **Two passes.** A first lane recorded this box's scripts this morning (07:59-10:14, `~/workspace/guacamole-research/ag8/`) and the Mac's hard reboot took its code before any commit; its recordings survived and are used, checked first: 55 set W replays on the O5b build from AF.8's fresh state (`bd1d8c0b…`) with the pristine Eruptor (`512d6e36…`) in a home of its own (`oracle-home-ag8`), screen locked, silent, `captures/BLUS30968/ag8-motion-1792423086/g1/` (30, 08:18-08:42, 1-minute load average 4.4-42.0) and `ag8-level-1792424606/` `la` (8, 08:43-08:49, 9.7-22.8), `lb` (6, 08:50-08:53, 5.5-18.6), `lc` (10, 08:54-09:01, 6.1-48.0) and `ld` (1, 09:03, 45.5-73.3, after 3 failed tries), `uptime` before and after each in its `record.json`. Record the stage's added wall time and size here (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: every one of the roster's 67 character load files settles a hero side, 0 listed, and Eruptor's walk and run off the IR skin within 1.3e-7 m of the decoders'. The rule (AE.5's slot-0 idle extended): the clips are the animation set's fixed slots, walk 1, jog 2, run 3, falling 57 and landing 61. The first four are `permanent/global.bld`'s own `AnimSlot_Walk`, `_Jog`, `_Run` and `_Fall` (read as an aid from ah-progression's `global_values.tsv`: Idle 0, Walk 1, Jog 2, Run 3, Fall 57, FlyUp 58, LeanLeft and LeanRight 12 and 13), which "Minion - Define Locomotion States" and "Minion - Initialize Locomotions" add as `animation palette [base + 1, 2, 3]` when the walk, jog or run speed isn't 0 (base 0 on foot, 90 flying); 61 is the slot the locomotion macro plays by number, layered, as a hero touches down. An empty slot is a gait the character lacks; a slot holding anything but a full NORMAL clip posing the skeleton at its start and halfway (an animation network, an additive or partial clip, a track past the skeleton) is refused by name; a character with neither a walk nor a run to move in, or no falling clip, is listed (`GUAC-E-HERO-UNSETTLED`) and keeps its model. The jog is taken beside the four the box names, by the same rule, so a script that sets a jog speed (AG.26) needs no re-extract. The hero is the `tfbActorInfo` drawing the portal model whose `CollisionInfo` blocks and meets the level (bits 3 and 4, `HERO_SOLID` 0x18; Stealth Elf's clone at 0x12 and Lightning Rod's upgrade avatar at 0x4 draw it too), else the one named as the load file, any case (Punch Pop Fizz alone: her `108_Alchemist_Red` draws `Alchemist_Legendary.mdl` where her portal sprite draws `AlchemistRed.mdl`, AE.20's question; her clips come from the portal model's set, the skeleton AE.6 extracted), exactly one or refused. Measured for decision 8's delay, off the dump: the archive read and parsed 2.5 s, the scene and props read 0.24 s, their RON 0.5 s; the rest of the stage's 10-11 s is AB's per-file `sync_all`, each file and each manifest save fsynced (the scene's and props' 696 texture files written alone: 7.57 s with it, 0.10 s without, at a load of 12); trading that durability for the first run's wait is AB's resume rule, so it's chotchki's call, not changed here. Fixed besides: `LevelStage::shared`'s doc said the characters', which on a resume counts the level's own textures too (289 of 289 on the 2-part resume), and `material::Track`'s doc said 4 tint tracks on the props where the disc has 3. No binary in the branch's diff (7 new files, all source), nothing tracked under an extract's extensions, the guard over the staged files, fmt, clippy -D warnings, test --workspace (1,316 passed, 0 failed, 112 ignored) and `openspec validate --all --strict` green; e2e: phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`, `E2E_APP=guacamole` 57 passed, 0 failed, 310 s at a 1-minute load of 16.8-9.7)

the speed after it holds to 1e-3 m/s). **The seam's gaps** (AG.4's review): handled in the controller: a plane's surface velocity (`proxy::Surfaces`, by `BodyId`, set by whoever moves the body; `guacamole`'s `MovingSolids`); a ray starting inside a convex (`ground::ray` goes on past where it leaves: parry's non-solid ray reports the exit with its normal facing back along the ray as a front face's would, not along it as the review guessed, so the tell is the ray's start overlapping the body it hit), used by the ground finder and the probe's sight line; the keep distance (casts stop 5 mm off the plane they hit, by that plane's own distance at the stop, so the cast's 1.6e-4 m overshoot and a stop at 0 both leave him at the keep: the synthetic wall's gap 5.000 mm at all six angles, 5.22 mm at 45° before); Avian seeing a kinematic move a step later (movers go after `HeroSystems::Step`, so both sides meet a body where it stood when the step began: the block test 0 m apart); the 0.001 m floor (`solid`, before the seam). On the Mac, release build: the time from the pick to the level loaded, and the worst TV frame through the load and at the level's first show, against level-scene's 50 ms and beside AE.17's floor, load average beside them (anything over gets a box of its own); the level at AF's fresh-state camera beside the oracle's screenshot agreeing by eye on layout (nothing mirrored; the gate, the waterfall and the fence where the screenshot has them), FLIP recorded, not gated, both images outside the tree (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: chapter 1 loads behind the portal in 1.86-2.32 s from the pick with no frame past 26.2 ms, and the dev entry draws it at the fresh state's camera where the oracle has it, nothing mirrored; one first-show frame on a cold shader cache took 53.0 ms, AG.42; fog's values are AG.41. **What's drawn** ([`guacamole::level_scene`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/level_scene.rs)): every tfbmulti2 segment of both scenes, the sky's 16 among them, as an unskinned mesh (`character::static_mesh`: the skinned mesh's attributes but joints and weights, so the warm's `MeshShape` gains `skinned` and a level key is its own pipeline), each material through AE's `CharacterMaterial::from_ir`; on level 213, 517 segments, 471 materials, 286 textures, 55 keys. Found on the way: most of the level's layered materials set no values for their layers (no third layer sets a blend; of the 177 second layers, 174 set no blend and 148 no exponent), which AE's material refused, so they take the defaults the June state's draws read in every patched copy bar those that set their own (second layer over at exponent 1 and detail 0.25, third multiplied: `ag19/notes/layer_defaults.txt`, over ae-shader's savestate check), written into this change's character-shading delta as "Layers a level material leaves unset" with design decision 18's row. **The light**: `_worldLight1`-`3` off the lights part, each travelling the way its `tfbEulerTransform` faces (AF.9's convention, AE.13's reading of the same angles on the sprite rig), colours decoded as the remaster decodes them, each with its own ambient, no multiplier applied (1 on the disc); the point light `213_EvilRobot_Beam_light` (visible, at the origin, about 103 m from the start) with its linear and quadratic attenuation taken as the shader's two falloff terms and its constant dropped, a reading (ae-shader's open item), under 1 % of its colour at the start; no shadows (AG.23). The shader lights in view space, so the level keeps its lights in the level's frame and lights every material for its camera, again whenever the camera moves (the view's uniform rebuilt, each scroll kept). A task reads the scene and lights parts (checked to be this dump's and level's), builds the meshes, decodes the textures and keys the draws; then each frame hands the renderer at most 16 MiB of textures and meshes, then 64 materials; the warm compiles the level's keys meanwhile, each a static triangle on its hidden stage in the level's scene, no glow mask, queued behind what's asked; the level spawns hidden on its own render layer and is ready only once the render world has every texture, mesh (in the allocator's buffers) and material (its bind group) resident and the warm has every key compiled. `/api/_test/tv` gains `level` (state, counts, timings, why it failed). **The dev entry**: `GUAC_LEVEL_VIEW` in a `dev-tools` build shows the ready level over the TV through the remaster's camera into the TV's own target (the window or the shots' image), at AF's fresh-state camera: AG.7's first run's `default` viewport at F0 ((6.51073, 52.266262, -107.12627), tilt -23.359688, heading 14.99966) and AC.13's projection of the same pose (0.6 mm off: 53.96° top to bottom, near 0.1) with camera 0's 800 ft far clip; it replaces the TV rather than blending over it, since Bevy alpha-blends a second camera on a target and the level's opaque parts write their maps' alpha (the first shot showed the portal through them). **Unit tests in this box**, green: `level_material_keys_become_their_shader_defs` (the box's first: the level's kinds of masks, ignore 0x2FF, 0x3FF and 0x3BFF, in the level's scene, their defs, the gloss map's bit off on both sides and refused when kept, another effect drawing nothing), `the_warm_list_equals_the_levels_keys` (the box's second: the keys the level asks the warm for are each once exactly those its entities draw, material key on mesh shape, the grass's not among them), `the_level_is_ready_only_once_every_part_is_resident` (the box's third: waiting with all 11 parts pending, then one texture, then the warm alone, ready only once all are struck off and every key compiled, hidden until shown), `a_level_without_its_world_lights_fails_and_says_why` (and another dump's parts refused), `the_dev_view_shows_the_level_and_a_moved_camera_relights_it` (the camera's place, layer and replacing write, the lights relit for a turned camera, a scroll held at its start under reduced motion), `the_world_lights_travel_the_way_they_face_and_the_caustic_stays_out`, `a_camera_sees_the_lights_in_its_view_space_and_the_fog_from_the_level`, `a_layer_with_no_values_takes_the_defaults`, `a_scene_draws_its_tfbmulti2_segments_and_keys_them_once` (two scenes in one list), `the_fresh_state_camera_looks_where_the_capture_does` (forward and up to AC.13's to 1e-4, 53.96°), `a_static_segment_becomes_a_mesh_without_joints`, and the warm triangle's test over both skins; Off the frame as claimed: the scene and lights parts are read, the meshes built (`static_mesh`), the textures decoded (`remaster::texture_view`) and the keys made on an `AsyncComputeTaskPool` task, and the frame only adds what the task built, 16 MiB of textures and meshes or 64 materials at a time; the materials are AE's `CharacterMaterial::from_ir`, lit and loaded through the `remaster` module the turntable uses, and drawn through its camera; every new file is source, the game-magic guard green over the index. (1) The residency gate went unseen: the ready test struck off every part before any key compiled, so `wait` with its residency check deleted still passed (bent, green); the test now compiles every key first and waits on residency alone, the other order is `a_resident_level_waits_on_its_keys`, and the render world's strike-off is a function of its own under `the_render_world_strikes_off_only_what_is_resident`. (2) A second source while the first still waited kept the first's pending ids, whose handles had gone with it, so it could never be ready: a new source clears them (`a_new_source_waits_on_its_own_parts_alone`). (3) The fog wasn't a resource: a field of the loader, fixed at the placeholder, nothing to change it after the build; `LevelFog` is now the TV's resource, initialised to the placeholder, each material made with it, a change relighting them all (`a_changed_fog_reaches_every_level_material`), so AG.41 sets one value (savestate-oracle's section 4 already has the June state's tutorial fog, (115, 83, 49) from 30.48 to 335.28 m on 2,397 copies, a check for AG.13's reading). One reading stated as more: the layer defaults' note (`ag19/notes/layer_defaults.txt`) tallies the June state's patched copies per variant without joining them to materials, so "bar those that set their own" is inferred, not shown: the third layer's multiply is on every copy, the second layer's over on most (0x1 3,890 of 5,166 copies, 0x41B 234 of 245), the rest unattributed. (4) The dev entry's first frame was lit for the identity: the relight ran in `Update` straight after the camera spawned, before its transform propagated, so the frame that first drew the level lit every material with the world's light directions as the view's and the next frame relit all 471 again; it now runs in `PostUpdate` after propagation and before the assets' events, from the fresh state's view as Bevy propagates it, and the show frame rewrites no material (the dev view's test checks both; it failed on the branch's code). Each check bent in turn failed its test: the residency gate, the keys gate, the stale ids, the strike's sense, the fog's change and its value, the relight back in the chain, a wrong lit-for. A removal, a second character, an item or an intro once the level shows are AG.29's: today the hero walks on, the cards stay under the level and an intro plays under the level's camera. `/api/_test/tv` gains `mode` (`portal`, `level`, `paused`) and `hero` (slot, figure, load file, his model's way to the level, and in the level his `position` and `heading`), and the level report `triangles`, `start` and `relit`; `GUAC_SHOT_WALK=<ms>` shoots the walk as a sequence (off by default: a shot's PNG is written on the frame's thread). **Found, and fixed here**: the level lit in view space (AG.19's review left it to this box) costs the walk 60 frames a second. Measured first on that build (`cold-1`, `warm-1`, below) the level ran at a 19.0 ms median frame standing as well as walking, against 16.7 ms shown and waiting: every frame the camera moved `light_level` rewrote all 471 of chapter 1's materials and the hero's, and at rest it still did, the eased camera's rotation jittering by float noise (131 of 144 frames at 144 Hz, headless, with the eye and his pivot unmoved). The character uniform's `misc.w` now says which frame its lights are in ([`material.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/character/material.rs) `LIGHTS_IN_WORLD`, [`shading.wesl`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/character/shading.wesl) `light_travel` and `point_position`): 1 hands the shader the directional lights' travels and the point lights' positions in the world's frame and it turns them by `view.view_from_world`, 0 keeps a turntable's rig in view space, as before. The level's and the hero's materials are made lit in the world's frame (`level_scene::world_scene`) and relit only when the fog changes; on that build the walk ran at 16.6-16.7 ms median frames, and the start's frame (`shots/cold-2/04-Portal-1cards-Ready.png` against `shots/cold-1/56-Portal-1cards-Ready.png`, the same pose) differs in 14,353 of its 6,220,800 channels, by 1 but for 60, which are 21 pixels on edges (32 at most), so the GPU turns the world's lights as the CPU did. **Unit tests in this box** (`cargo test -p guacamole`, headless, made-up data): the box's three, `a_character_placed_enters` (Eruptor placed with the level loaded: the level shows through the following camera, order 1 with the replacing write on the level's layer, the pads the level's; the first pad's stick walks him along the camera's 15° within 0.05° and the made-up wall 6 m ahead holds him 5 mm off it, his 0.8644 m sphere on the triangles' 0.05 m, while he slides on along it past 3 m; Start pauses), `with_the_level_still_loading_the_portal_view_holds_then_enters` (his model ready and no level: the portal view for 30 frames; the level loaded under his intro: the portal view for 30 more; the intro skipped: in) and `the_start_pose` (placed before the level loads, so he walks in the frame its floor spawns: the waypoint's x and z, settled 5 mm over the floor within 1 mm, facing 0°, Eruptor's tuning and solid; the eye (−3.679547, 8.883567, −13.732257) off his feet within 1e-4 m, the view along 15° and 23.360138° down within 1e-4°; his model on him at 1.5 on the level's layer; no pad: paused for one and unmoved); and `another_character_keeps_the_portal_view_and_leaving_the_portal_leaves_the_level`, `a_hero_taken_off_before_the_level_shows_never_enters`, `a_following_camera_lights_nothing_again` (144 frames at rest and 144 walking at 144 Hz relight nothing, every draw on the level's layer lit in the world's frame); in the library `the_start_is_the_init_locations_first_waypoint`, `the_levels_collision_becomes_static_bodies`, `the_hero_draws_in_the_levels_light_and_fog_without_its_point_light` and `eruptor_walks_on_his_live_numbers_and_no_one_else_yet`; In AG.34's walk-W-06 the ring's record at flip 1,729 (read 0, game frame 7,690, 191 ms after the record before it, the game frames one apart throughout) has no O4 count (`PAD_STATE 0 1729` answered `no_read`) and flip 1,730 has no record; the runner asks O4 only about flips the ring holds, so whether O4 kept that read under 1,730 or not at all isn't known. `RingCheck` counted it (`reads_mismatch` 1, `holds` false): 1 read of AG.34's 18,000 watched, none of AG.35's 36,600. A record's guest memory (pose, stick floats, `_curFrame`) is read at that read whatever its flip says; what a split key moves is O4's readback joined to the record (`pad` none there) and any script step mapped to a game frame through a record's flip. Options: take the counter once at the top of `pad_get_data` and hand it to both (O5c, a new build); or put O4's flip in O5's header beside its own. Either way, the runner asks O4 about every flip in the ring's span, so a read O4 kept under the next flip, or never kept, shows as which. Its loopback case: a flip ticking between the two loads. Closes before AG.8 takes a frame's input from O4's readback or from a script's flips rather than from the record's own stick floats. On the three planes (2 each): walking down 1.32-2.54 m/s, across 0.01-0.52, up 1.21-2.73, each uphill slide leaving the ground for a frame (whose 10 ft/s turns the upward vertical part down), 0.81-1.05 m in 0.23-0.27 s; the vertical part is the slope's (6.39 ft/s, 21.5 × sin 17.3°, walking down t2979) or a crest's. ## Workflow follow-up

- Archive only after AA and AE have archived: AG's figure-cards and figure-turntable deltas MODIFY requirements that live in their unarchived deltas, and its character-shading delta adds to AE's unarchived capability, so `openspec archive` would refuse AG first. A MODIFIED block replaces the whole requirement at archive, so before AG archives each one is rebuilt from AA's and AE's archived text with AG's scoping applied, and diffed against what AG carries, so no later AA or AE edit (AE.13's, say) is silently reverted (AG.0 records the diff).
- AE.13 (the turntables on the cards) and AG.29 (the level taking the TV from the cards) change the same screen; whichever lands second keeps the other's tests passing.
